# 🐭 Mouse Group

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

Mouse Groups are groups of mice which have similar weaknesses or exist in similar locations. No mouse is currently in more than one mouse group, even if that mouse exists in many locations. The mice of each group are listed roughly in order of weakest to strongest.

Please see the Effectiveness page for detailed information on which mice are weak to which trap Power Types, as not all mice in the same group have the exact same weaknesses.

## Mouse Groups

### Indigenous Mice

Known Weaknesses: Physical, Shadow, Tactical, Arcane, Forgotten, Hydro, Draconic, Law, Rift

Indigenous mice live in the core hunting areas, such as Gnawnia, The Harbor and The Mountain. Physical traps work best against indigenous mice, and they prefer normal flavours of cheese, such as cheddar, marble, swiss, brie or SUPER|brie+.

A list of Indigenous Mice can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups:

| Misc.<br>Shiny Seekers<br>Great Gnawnian Games; and<br>Rare Rodent |
| --- |

### Dock Dwellers

Known Weaknesses: Physical, Shadow, Tactical, Arcane, Forgotten, Hydro, Draconic, Law

Blimey! These pirates are frequently found harassing ships at the Harbour in search of valuable cargo to steal.

A list of Dock Dwellers can be found here.

### Mountain Mice

Known Weaknesses: Physical, Shadow, Tactical, Arcane, Forgotten, Hydro, Draconic, Law

Although these mice originate from the mountain range in Gnawnia, a few of these breeds can occasionally be found in other locations.

A list of Mountain Mice can be found here.

### Forest Guild

Known Weaknesses: Physical, Shadow, Tactical, Arcane, Forgotten, Hydro, Draconic, Law

Members of the Forest Guild dwell in Whisker Woods, and their traits reflect their green surroundings. Physical and tactical weapons work quite well on members of the Guild, but normal bait may not always work. While weaker Forest Guild members enjoy normal cheese, the powerful Silth mouse will only respond to something. . . extremely gnarly.

A list of Forest Guild mice can be found here.

### Lab Experiments

Known Weaknesses: Physical, Shadow, Tactical, Arcane, Forgotten, Hydro, Law

The various radioactive materials at the Laboratory have mutated some of these mice into powerful adversaries! Pay special care when hunting the fearsome Monster Mouse!

A list of Lab Experiments mice can be found here.

### The Shadow Clan

Known Weaknesses: Shadow, Arcane, Forgotten

These spoo-oo-ky mice like to shamble around graveyards and catacombs, looking for their favorite snack, Radioactive Blue Cheese. If you've come to the graveyard in search of the infamous Lycan Mouse, however, Moon Cheese might just make it howl.

A list of The Shadow Clan mice can be found here.

### Digby Dirt Dwellers

Known Weaknesses: Physical, Shadow, Tactical, Arcane

The Digby Dirt Dwellers all share a love of subterranean life, whether they're building tunnels, hanging out in the sediment, or just picking up shiny stones they find on the ground. They are weak against physical traps, and are only attracted to the subtle vibration of Limelight Cheese.

A list of Digby Dirt Dwellers can be found here.

### Followers of Furoma

Known Weaknesses: Physical, Tactical

These martial mice are weak to tactical traps. Most of them are attracted to normal cheese, but if Hunters want to navigate the Dojo, there are some things they should remember. Students will leave their training for Maki cheese any day, but Masters are only attracted to specific cheese made from the tokens dropped by students. As for the Master of the Dojo, Hunters should try hard to make his stomach rumble. Still looking for a challenge after defeating the Master? Bait your trap with the rare Onyx Gorgonzola, and you might meet the Sensei himself.

A list of Followers of Furoma can be found here.

### The Forgotten Mice

Known Weaknesses: Shadow, Arcane, Forgotten

Forgotten Mice have served the will of the Acolyte Mouse for untold centuries, working to release him from his prison in the Acolyte Realm. Forgotten minions prefer Radioactive Blue or Ancient Cheese, but the overlords of the Acolyte Realm are only attracted to Runic Cheese.

A list of The Forgotten Mice can be found here.

### Aquatic Order

Known Weaknesses: Physical, Tactical, Hydro

The Aquatic Order inhabits the vast blue seas of Rodentia, and a few other related waterways around Gnawnia. Normal cheese will suffice to tempt them out of the deeps, but make sure to equip a Hydro trap, or the Aquatic mouse you're after may well end up being the one that got away.

A list of Aquatic Order mice can be found here.

### The Elub Tribe

Known Weaknesses: Hydro

Notable for their blue garments and love of the seashore, the Elub Tribe can only be caught using Hydro traps. Normal cheese does not interest them, so keep plenty of Gouda or SUPER|brie+ on hand. The village elders are quite elusive, but rumor has it that Shell Cheese can bring them out of hiding.

A list of The Elub Tribe mice can be found here.

### The Nerg Tribe

Known Weaknesses: Tactical

The Nerg tribe are masters of disguise, and only a tactical trap can penetrate their defenses. The younger tribe members prefer Gouda or SUPER|brie+, but the elders will only pit themselves against your trap if a piece of Gumbo cheese is on the line.

A list of The Nerg Tribe mice can be found here.

### The Derr Tribe

Known Weaknesses: Physical

The Derr Tribe have managed to survive for centuries in a harsh desert, growing stronger to combat the dry wind and the heat. As a result, only Physical traps can hold them. The tribes-mice of Derr enjoy Gouda or SUPER|brie+, but Crunchy cheese is required to draw the attention of the tribal elders.

A list of The Derr Tribe mice can be found here.

### The Dreaded Horde

Known Weaknesses: Shadow

These monstrous mice live deep in the Jungle of dread. Horde members respond only to Havarti, and each mouse prefers a different spice mixed in with their cheese. The Horde may be a picky bunch, but catching them is well worth the effort, for they are the guardians of the parts to an ancient trap capable of catching mice older than Gnawnia itself!

A list of The Dreaded Horde mice can be found here.

### Draconic Brood

Known Weaknesses: Draconic

From the depths of the volcanic rock around Dracano spring the Draconic Brood, a rare and dangerous group of mice who share a love for fire and destruction. A Draconic type trap, equipped with insanely spicy Inferno Havarti Cheese, has the best chance of ending a Draconic mouse's rampage.

A list of Draconic Brood mice can be found here.

### Balack's Banished

Known Weaknesses: Shadow, Arcane, Forgotten

These accursed mice have been twisted by the dark enchantment surrounding Balack's Cove. The sweet smell of Vanilla Stilton will draw them in from beyond the grave, but if you seek the Lich Elders, or Balack himself, Vengeful Vanilla Stilton will give your trap extra bite. The Banished can be caught using Shadow, Arcane, or Forgotten traps, but Forgotten traps work best against Balack and his Elder minions.

A list of Balack's Banished mice can be found here.

### Gauntlet Gladiators

Known Weaknesses: none officially listed

The legion of mice swarming the King's Gauntlet comes from diverse and untold regions, and therefore their individual taste in cheese, and their power type differs as greatly as their lands of origin. Hunters seeking to take their trapping skills to the next level should look for special potion drops from the mice they are currently hunting. Only then will they possess the proper bait (and the proper knowledge of power types) to reach the top.

A list of Gauntlet Gladiators mice can be found here. The mice in this group are further divided into sub-groups, called Tiers:

| Tier 1: Puppet<br>Tier 2: Thief<br>Tier 3: Melee<br>Tier 4: Bard | Tier 5: Magic<br>Tier 6: Noble<br>Tier 7: Dust<br>Tier 8: The Eclipse |
| --- | --- |

### Seasonal Soldiers

Known Weaknesses: Tactical

From the ever-changing vistas of Zugzwang's garden come the Seasonal Soldiers, a group of mice who have been infused with the magic of the four seasons. If you want to draw these 'seasoned' veterans in, use normal cheeses, such as swiss, brie, gouda or SUPER|brie+ and a tactical trap, but know this: The Hunter seeking true mastery of the Seasonal Soldiers must find the special weaknesses that are unique to each season.

A list of Seasonal Soldiers can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on the season the Seasonal Garden is in:

| Winter<br>Spring<br>Summer; and<br>Fall |
| --- |

### Wizard's Pieces

Known Weaknesses: Tactical

Fighting a perpetual battle within the enchanted halls of Zugzwang's tower are the Wizard's Pieces, warrior mice who follow a complicated and rigid set of rules in order to achieve victory. On one side is the Technic army, brimming with metalworks and gadgets, and on the other, the Mystic army, who use spells and charms to gain the upper hand. You'll have to defeat the entire chess set to reach the king, so plan your moves carefully!

A list of Wizard's Pieces mice can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups:

| Misc<br>Mystic; and<br>Technic |
| --- |

### Zurreal's Breed

Known Weaknesses: Physical, Tactical

This group of mice have specifically adapted to infiltrate Zugzwang's Library. Their non-organic mix of magic and technology allow them to pass safely through the library's specialized security where they are keen to tear the almost infinite amount of research to shreds.

A list of Zurreal's Breed mice can be found here.

### Icewing's Invasion

Known Weaknesses: Hydro

Swarming down from the icy north, these mice are pounding on the very walls of Gnawnia's castle! While they're all repulsed by Hydro weapons (who likes being wet when it's cold?), they seems to have a particular weakness to hot Hydro weapons. Arm yourself accordingly and head out, hunters!

A list of Icewing's Invasion mice can be found here. The mice in this group are further divided into sub-groups, based on how deep the Hunter travels through the Iceberg:

| Misc.<br>Bergling<br>Tunnel Rat<br>Brute | Bomb Squad<br>Zealot; and<br>Icewing's Generals<br>*nocontent |
| --- | --- |

### Wild Bunch

Known Weaknesses: Law

Hardy and untamed, these varmints of the Claw Shot City have adapted to the harsh conditions of the wild frontier. It takes strong will and independence to survive far from the cozy confines of city life, and these mice have grown accustomed to the freedom from the Law.

A list of Wild Bunch mice can be found here. The mice in this group are further divided into sub-groups:

| Misc.<br>Crew; and<br>Ringleader |
| --- |

### Train Robbers

Known Weaknesses: Law

These mice have one thing on their minds: Raid the supplies on the Gnawnian Express or prevent it from completing its trip. Nothing else matters to these lawless mice and they will fight to sabotage the train at all costs!

A list of Train Robbers mice can be found here. The mice in this group are further divided into sub-groups, based on the different phases of the train ride:

| Passenger<br>Depot Worker<br>Automice<br>Raider; and<br>Fueler |
| --- |

### Meteorite Miners

Known Weaknesses: Shadow, Arcane, Law

The unfortunate mice caught up within the magic and mayhem of the fallen meteor around Fort Rox. Rough by day, terrifying by night!

A list of Meteorite Miners mice can be found here. The mice in this group are further divided into sub-groups, based on how late the Hunter hunts through the Night:

| Misc.<br>Weremice<br>Cosmic Critter<br>Special; and<br>Dawn Destroyer |
| --- |

### The Marching Flame

Known Weaknesses: Physical, Tactical, Hydro

All that lies in the path of The Marching Flame is destroyed. This force of mice has marched the desert for years conquering land and destroying settlements.

A list of The Marching Flame mice can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on the type of fighting unit they belong to:

| Archer<br>Artillery<br>Cavalry<br>Mage | Scout<br>Warrior<br>Support; and<br>Command |
| --- | --- |

### Muridae Market Mice

Known Weaknesses: Physical, Tactical, Law

In wake of the Marching Flame's attack, these mice have taken claim on the Muridae Market. Rebuilding damaged shops has proven quite difficult due to these mice continuously stealing building materials as soon as they are delivered.

A list of the Muridae Market mice can be found here.

### Living Garden Mice

Known Weaknesses: Hydro

The armies of the Living Garden have really taken advantage of their environment, putting the local flora to use in crafting weapons and armour. But why are they gathering in such numbers?

A list of Living Garden mice can be found here.

### Lost City Mice

Known Weaknesses: Arcane

These mice were subjected to a terrible curse that has affected them all equally and terribly. However, nobody knows where the curse came from or who exactly was responsible and recriminations abound.

A list of Lost City mice can be found here.

### Sand Dunes Mice

Known Weaknesses: Shadow

Only tough and hardy mice can survive the dunes, but even they are wary and cautious around the ravenous Grubling Mice. A tenuous ecosystem flourishes in the dunes, where predators and prey both seek out food while avoiding dangerous stampedes.

A list of Sand Dunes mice can be found here.

### Queso Canyoneers

Known Weaknesses: Shadow, Arcane, Draconic, Law

Swimming in a sea of molten Queso, this vast canyon of smooth, buttery, liquid cheese has attracted a variety of spice loving mice looking for that perfect blend of Queso Cheese. Recently, dragon-like mice have joined these mice in their quest for queso.

A list of Queso Canyoneers mice can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on the different stages and cheese used in each location:

| River Riders<br>Spice Mice<br>Quarry Quarries | Cork Collector<br>Pressure Builder; and<br>Geyser Hunter |
| --- | --- |

### Deep Sea Dwellers

Known Weaknesses: Hydro

Deep beneath the surface of the Rodentia Ocean lie the Deep Sea Dwellers. Centered around the Sunken City, there is an entire civilization of mice, isolated from the outside world, waiting to be discovered!

A list of Deep Sea Dwellers mice can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on how deep they are likely to be found in relation to Sunken City, and what loot they are likely to drop:

| Sunken City Citizens<br>Finned Fiend<br>Coral Corral<br>Barnacled Bunch | Scale Society<br>Treasure Troop; and<br>Predator Pack<br>*nocontent |
| --- | --- |

### Fungal Fiends

Known Weaknesses: Forgotten, Hydro

Crawling forth from the deep, dark and dank caves and caverns below are a few unique breeds of mice descending from a long line of rocks, crystals and fungus. Strange, marvelous and powerful! Creep carefully when crawling through these caverns!

A list of Fungal Fiends can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on how deep they are in the Fungal Cavern:

| Fungal Fodder<br>Gruyere Grazer<br>Mineral Muncher<br>Gemstone Gorger; and<br>Diamond Devourer |
| --- |

### Citizens of Zokor

Known Weaknesses: Arcane, Forgotten

Antiquated mice from an antiquated era. Locked away behind the lost Labyrinth, these mice have continued their existence protecting their city. If the Labyrinth doesn't get you, the mice will!

A list of the Citizens of Zokor can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on the Labyrinth hallways and Zokor districts they reside in:

| Hallway Wanderer<br>Fungal Farmer<br>Lost Scholar<br>Fealty Sworn Soldier | Tech Engineer<br>Treasure Miser; and<br>Hidden Remnant |
| --- | --- |

### Moussu Picchu Inhabitants

Known Weaknesses: Arcane, Draconic, Shadow

Hidden away for years by the mountain ranges south of the Hollow Heights, these mice threaten to unleash a perfect storm of mischief should they ever advance towards Gnawnia.

A list of the Moussu Picchu Inhabitants mice can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on weather intensity of Moussu Picchu and cheese used:

| Fungal Feeder<br>Potion Brewer<br>Wind Wanderer<br>Rain Roamer; and<br>Storm Dragon |
| --- |

### Floating Islanders

Known Weaknesses: Physical, Shadow, Tactical, Arcane, Forgotten, Hydro, Draconic, Law

High above the tallest mountain peaks, you'll find these light-hearted islanders basking in the sun. They lead a thrilling life of cloud chasing, island hopping, and high-altitude treasure hunting.

A list of Floating Islanders mice can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on the islands they reside in and power types:

| Launch Pad<br>Cloud Commoner<br>Physical Pummeler<br>Shadow Overcaster | Tactical Dog Fighter<br>Atmospheric Arcane<br>Floating Forgotten<br>Hovering Hydro | Dashing Dragon<br>Lofty Lawbreaker<br>Sky Pirate<br>Sky Warden | Sky Paragon<br>Palace Protectors<br>The Richest; and<br>Empyrean Guard |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |

### Foreword Farmers

Known Weaknessess: Tactical

Some may call these mice the "Salt of the Earth" but a "Thorn in Your Side" would be more accurate. These pesky critters enjoy nothing more than digging in the dirt, nibbling on valuable crops, and preventing hard-working hunters from reaping the rewards of this farm's enchanted soil! Any standard cheese will lure them away from their crop-crunching but it'll take a strong Tactical trap to clear the farm of these persistent pests.

A list of Foreword Farmers can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on the number of plants planted:

| Seed Stowers; and<br>Petulant Pests |
| --- |

### Prologue Pond Prowlers

Known Weakness: Hydro

This is not your typical fishing trip! These fresh water aquatic antagonists have a unique appetite for various types of clever but peculiar cheeses crafted from the items found in their pond. Make sure to load your boat with a hefty Hydro trap if you plan to venture out onto this precarious pond!

A list of Prologue Pond Prowlers can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on the cheese used:

| Grub Gatherers<br>Shallow Swimmers; and<br>Deep Divers |
| --- |

### Storytellers

Known Weaknessess: Forgotten

This collection of creative characters is composed of the greatest authors ever to walk on four legs in addition to many of their most famous and infamous storybook characters. At the helm of this malicious magnum opus is the Mythweaver himself who is using these authors and their characters to rewrite the history of Gnawnia! Arm yourself with an assortment of Draft Derby cheese, a powerful Forgotten trap, and a great deal of ingenuity before facing off against these verbose villains!

A list of Storytellers can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on the cheese used and the number of words written:

| Folklore Authors<br>Folklore Characters; and<br>Folklore Masterminds |
| --- |

### Beanstalkers

Known Weaknessess: Physical

From the bottom of the beanstalk all the way up to the Castle in the Clouds above, you'll find a vast assortment of challenging but valuable rodents. Arm yourself with an impressive Physical trap before climbing this stalk because whether you're facing guards in the Dungeon, socialites in the Ballroom, or nobles in the Great Hall, you've got a challenge ahead of you! Most of all, beware the wrath of the Giants because they don't take kindly to unwanted strangers waking them from their slumber!

A list of Beanstalkers can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on the vine planted:

| Budding Baddies<br>Dungeon Dwellers<br>Ballroom Blitzers; and<br>Ruthless Royals |
| --- |

### Sorcery Apprentices

Known Weaknessess: Arcane, Shadow

The Mythweaver has been training a new generation of magical mice in the ways of Arcane and Shadow sorcery at this enchanted institution.

A list of Sorcery Apprentices can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on the course taken:

| Monitor of the Halls<br>Arcane Academics<br>Master Sorcerers; and<br>Shadow Scholars |
| --- |

### Dragons of the Depths

Known Weaknessess: Draconic

Buried beneath the surface of Gnawnia lies a vast realm of ancient caverns inhabited by serpentine monsters that have remained hidden for untold centuries. These terrifying creatures have surfaced only occasionally in the past, taking control of whatever region they emerged from. But now that their underground lair has been exposed, these elemental enemies are eager to expand their horizons, take flight, and dominate the world above.

A list of Dragons of the Depths can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups, based on the cavern type the mice reside in:

| Fire Dragons<br>Ice Dragons<br>Poison Dragons; and<br>Elemental Dragons |
| --- |

### Afterword Anti-Arborists

Known Weaknessess: Tactical

This invasive species of arboreal mice are spreading a wretched blight throughout the Afterword Acres in an attempt to poison and destroy the Infinitree.

A list of Afterword Anti-Arborists can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups:

| Foliage Fomenters<br>Brushwood Bruisers<br>Deciduous Destroyers<br>Coniferous Corrupters; and<br>Blight Bringers |
| --- |

### Epilogue Ecosystem

Known Weaknessess: Hydro

These mischevious, marine mice are a menace to the ecosystem and they seem intent on guarding the glorious and gargantuan falls at the end of the rapids.

A list of Epilogue Ecosystem mice can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups:

| Coastal Crawlers<br>Slow Sea Shrubs<br>Agile Algae Eaters<br>Speedy Shellfish; and<br>Torrential Tyrants |
| --- |

### Conclusion Cliffhangers

Known Weaknessess: Law

These infamous characters from throughout Gnawnian literature, spanning multiple works and genres, are the Mythweaver's final weapon against the MouseHunters. Conclude the Mythweaver's story by clashing with this colourful cast of characters and correcting their content conclusively.

A list of Conclusion Cliffhangers mice can be found here. These mice are further divided into sub-groups:

| Prose Proprietors<br>Adventurous Adversaries<br>Comedic Caricatures<br>Romantic Rivals<br>Suspenseful Scoundrels<br>Tragic Troublemakers<br>Fantasy Fanatics<br>Lore Weavers |
| --- |

### Rift Walkers

Known Weaknesses: Rift

Located in another dimension, the Gnawnia Rift Walkers are the supreme archetypes of the Gnawnian mice. Surrounded by swirls of Rift energy, they possess power beyond belief. Even the greatest of mouse-hunting technology poses little threat to these magical, mysterious mice. To all MouseHunters seeking to challenge these mice: Steel yourselves, bring your courage, and do not let fear overcome you.

A list of Rift Walkers mice can be found here. The mice in this group are further divided into sub-groups, based on the locations the mice reside in:

| Gnawnia Rift<br>Burroughs Rift; and<br>Whisker Woods Rift |
| --- |

### Rift Stalkers

Known Weaknesses: Rift

The Rift Stalkers are terrifying predators from beyond the Rift, exemplars of their kind. Far more powerful than the previously-encountered Rift Walkers. The only things that can harm them are specially-crafted Rift traps. Make sure you're properly equipped before taking on these deadly mice!

A list of Rift Stalkers mice can be found here. The mice in this group are further divided into sub-groups, based on the locations the mice reside in:

| Furoma Rift<br>Bristle Woods Rift; and<br>Valour Rift |
| --- |

### The Polluted

Known Weaknesses: Hydro

Sticky, Gross, Smelly and Strange. These mice have been exposed to the evils of the Laboratory for far too long and have now developed some interesting side effects! Whether it be from the pollution or the repeated strange experiments they were put through, you wont believe them until you see them!

A list of The Polluted mice can be found here.

### Event Mice

Known Weaknesses: Physical, Shadow, Tactical, Arcane, Forgotten, Hydro, Draconic, Law, Rift

These mice are released occasionally during various parts of the year. Each is fairly unique, often requiring a specific hunting strategy.

A list of Event Mice can be found here. The mice in this group are further divided into sub-groups:

| Lunar New Year<br>Birthday<br>Spring Egg Hunt<br>Halloween | Great Winter Hunt<br>New Year<br>Prize<br>Ronza's Stowaways |
| --- | --- |

## Preferred Cheese

The Mouse With No Name has no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Law type traps such as S.L.A.C. are very effective against the Mouse With No Name. They are more attracted when the correct charm (Roof Rack, Door Guard, or Greasy Glob) for the current wave is armed, and do not visit during Trap Checks.

## Event Participation

- The Mouse With No Name drops the Gnawnian Express Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Released on 26 September 2013 as part of the Gnawnian Express Station content.
- The name of this mouse is a reference to the "Man With No Name" character played by Clint Eastwood in a series Sergio Leone Spaghetti Westerns.

## Locations

Hunters can encounter the Mousevina von Vermin in the Mousoleum only.

Hunters must capture the Mousevina von Vermin in the Mousoleum location in order to obtain the Shredded Furoma Map Piece.

The Shredded Furoma Map Piece allows Hunters to access the Training Grounds location.

Hunters can take the Shredded Furoma Map Piece to the Bazaar Cartographer to repair it and obtain the Stitched Furoma Map Piece which allows Hunters to access the Dojo, Meditation Room, and Pinnacle Chamber locations.

## History and Trivia

- 19 March 2014: The Moussile Mouse was released as part of MouseHunt's Sixth Birthday event.
- The first capture of the Moussile Mouse awarded the hunter with the Limited Edition collectible Inert Riftwood, along with this bonus message:

> Capturing a Moussile Mouse revealed another clue!
I found a fragment of material that has faint traces of the same type of energy now being produced at the power generator.
The metal seems to fade in and out, as though it's from another plane of existence. Could this be evidence of the twisted world spilling further into our own? Could this be some sort of a rift opening?!
Clearly, the Moussiles are to blame for the generator sabotage!
Convinced I've learned all there is to know for now, the King gave me a reward!
30 King's Credits have been added to my inventory.

- Hunters that obtained the Inert Riftwood had the option of purchasing the Rift Detector map piece early at the Vacant Lot Cartographer.
- 25 March 2014: The Moussile Mouse became the first Event Mouse to remain available in the Vacant Lot after an event ended.

> After MouseHunt's Sixth Birthday event ended, the Moussile Mouse dropped Inert Riftwood in the Vacant Lot but no longer dropped other loot.

# 🐭 Mutated Behemoth Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Mutated Behemoth Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Toxic Spill, for hunters of rank Archduke/Archduchess and above.

| Mutated Behemoth Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: The Polluted |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 150,000 | Gold: | 12,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |

| Locations: | Toxic Spill | Loot: | Crude Pollutinum Divine Orb Polluted Base Blueprints |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| This is him, the mouse that started it all. Caught on camera at ground zero, near the state-of-the-art filtration system. From within the Laboratory, he followed his nose through the complex system of pipes and passages towards the major outflow filter... which was covered with Radioactive Blue cheese runoff! He had escaped from his containment at the Laboratory and was searching desperately for food.That heavenly "blue cheese" filter probably looked like the biggest piece of cheese this tiny little one had ever seen! Without a moment's hesitation, he began to chew through the whole filtration system! Full of harmful chemicals, toxins and the King knows what else, once destroyed there was nothing left to stop the contaminated outflow!This mouse continued eating and eating and growing and eating as he was pushed out through the tunnel, unable to stop! The radiation and toxins drastically increased his size and appetite! The King fears that this mouse, now insatiable and of epic proportions, will never be stopped if the pollution isn't cleaned up soon... |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 521 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Mutated Behemoth mice are especially attracted to Rancid Radioactive Blue cheese, to the point that any other kind of cheese will simply not suffice.

Hydro type traps are effective against the Mutated Behemoth Mouse.

## Event Participation

- The Mutated Behemoth Mouse drops the Toxic Spill Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.
- The Mutated Behemoth Mouse drops the Mutated Behemoth Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

| Journal Excerpt #2137, 7th District, Area A6:<br>"Radiation has no effect on the brown mice... or so we thought!<br>We knew they were much more craftier than their white and grey counterparts but we did not realize the oh-so-minute changes after initial experimentation.<br>They got faster, stronger, smarter and much more dexterous. <br>Due to their exceptional genetics, it took years for the radiation to hit critical mass and finally we now see the results of our years of testing. ## Cheese and Charm Preference

### Cheese

In the Acolyte Realm and Forbidden Grove locations, the Mutated Brown Mouse is attracted only to Radioactive Blue Cheese.
In the Bazaar location, the Mutated Brown Mouse is attracted to Standard Cheese and Gilded Cheese. 
In the Laboratory location, the Mutated Brown Mouse is attracted to Radioactive Blue Cheese, Rancid Radioactive Blue Cheese and Magical Rancid Radioactive Blue Cheese.

### Charm

No Charms are required to attract the Mutated Brown Mouse.

## Power Type Weaknesses

The Mutated Brown Mouse is weak to all Power Types (except Draconic, Parental and Rift). However, in the Forbidden Grove, and Acolyte Realm locations Shadow or Arcane weapons will be effective against a larger group of the available mice.
It is recommended that Hunters use a trap that will be effective against the main mouse population in their hunting location.

| VeryEffective | Effective | Less Effective |
| --- | --- | --- |
| None | PhysicalShadowTacticalArcaneForgottenHydroLaw | None |

Hunters can encounter the Mutated Brown Mouse in the following locations:

- Burroughs:

> Laboratory
Bazaar

- Bristle Woods

> Forbidden Grove
Acolyte Realm

## Loot

The Mutated Brown Mouse drops Stale Cheese as loot in the Forbidden Grove and Acolyte Realm locations.

The Mutated Brown Mouse is not known to drop any loot in the Laboratory or Bazaar locations.

## Event Participation

Attraction info and special event loot drops for the Mutated Brown Mouse can be found at the individual event location pages.

### 2015

- Great Winter Hunt 2015

### 2014

- Halloween 2014

### 2013

- Halloween 2013

## History and Trivia

- 17 October 2013: The Mutated Brown Mouse was released at the start of the Halloween 2013 event.
- 17 October 2013: The Mutated Brown Mouse was released to all the same locations as the Mutated White Mouse and Mutated Grey Mouse.
- 25 March 2014: The Mutated Brown Mouse gained a vulnerability to Rift power type traps, with the introduction of the Rift Plane.
- 9 September 2014: The Amplified Brown Mouse mouse was introduced as the Rift counterpart of the Mutated Brown Mouse.
- 17 October 2018: The Mutated Brown Mouse changed mouse groups from Indigenous Mice to Lab Experiments and also was removed from the Mousoleum. It is also no longer attracted to Standard Cheese in Laboratory.

| Looking upon this mouse, it may appear to have physical form, but it is cold and void of life. It does not eat cheese, it merely senses your cheese and hunts you down if you wander too closely to its home.<br>The story goes... There was once a scholar from Zokor who created magical guardians to protect the city's mysterious Dark Library. These guardians were powerful and kept the library safe... Too safe. This scholar, thinking he could claim the entire Scholar District for his own, commanded his guardians to prevent anyone from coming near the grounds. Much devastation was wrought and some of the knowledge stored within the library was lost before he could be stopped. When his plan was finally foiled, as punishment, his material form was divided and forever bound to his constructs in order to repay the city for the countless years of knowledge that was forever lost. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 776 |  |  |

The Mystic Guardian Mouse prefers Glowing Gruyere in both the Labyrinth and Zokor, but can be attracted with Standard Cheeses.

The Mystic Herald Mouse prefers Glowing Gruyere in both the Labyrinth and Zokor, but can be attracted with Standard Cheeses.

# 🐭 Mystic King Mouse

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

The Mystic King Mouse is a breed of mouse found in the Rodentia region. It is known to drop Mystic Curd, Mystic Crystal, Torn Mystic Page and Hunting Research.

| Mystic King Mouse |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Wizard's Pieces (Mystic) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 98,670 | Gold: | 8,560 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Tactical | Other Requirements: | None |
| Cheese: | No Preference | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Zugzwang's Tower | Loot: | Hunting Research (10,000)Mystic CrystalMystic CurdTorn Mystic Page |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Preferring to hover over the battlefield rather than recline on his throne, the Mystic King commands his armies with the force of his mind. Many Hunters assume that the Mystic King can see what move they are going to make before they make it, but this is not the case. The Mystic King's vast intellect and store of strategic wisdom simply allows him to see all possible moves before they are made, which is why he considers every move carefully before acting. |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 254 |  |  |

## Preferred Cheese

Mystic King mice have no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

The weaknesses of the Mystic King mouse differ slightly from most others in the Wizard's Pieces group. Tactical weapons are very effective against it, while all others are ineffective. It is only available to attract after a Mystic Queen mouse has been caught.

Upon capturing a Mystic King mouse, the hunter is automatically transported back to the Seasonal Garden unless they are using either Checkmate Cheese or a Stalemate Charm. By using either Checkmate Cheese or a Stalemate Charm, a hunter is able to remain in Zugzwang's Tower and attempt to capture additional mice, including the Chess Master.

Upon capturing a Mystic King mouse with either Checkmate Cheese or a Stalemate Charm armed, the mouse population changes. If Checkmate Cheese is armed, the available mice will be the Chess Master, Mystic Rook and whichever Technic mice have been unlocked. If Standard Cheese is armed, the available mice will be the Mystic Pawn and whichever Technic mice have been unlocked.

## Event Participation

### Spring Egg Hunt

- The Mystic King Mouse drops the Mystic King Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

## History and Trivia

- Catching the Mystic King Mouse became one requirement for traveling to the Fiery Warpath when it was released on 22 March 2011. Hunters who had already caught this mouse did not have to catch another one.
- Mystic King mice began dropping the Antique Scalloped Pink Egg as loot, on 19 April 2011, for the Spring Egg Hunt 2011 event.
- 29 July 2012: Catching the Mystic King Mouse is no longer a requirement for gaining the Sandtail Call to Arms

Mystic Rook mice have no specific preference for any kind of cheese.

The Mystic Rook mouse is a typical member of the Wizard's Pieces group, and as such, Tactical weapons are of normal effectiveness against it, while all others are ineffective. They are only available to attract after 2 Mystic Bishop mice have been caught.

- The Mystic Rook Mouse drops the Zugzwang's Egg during the annual Spring Egg Hunt.

# 🐭 Mythical Dragon Emperor

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

Mythical Dragon Emperor is a breed of mouse found on the in Draconic Depths.

| Mythical Dragon Emperor |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Dragons of the Depths (Elemental Dragons) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 200,000 | Gold: | 120,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Draconic | Other Requirements: |  |
| Cheese: | Elemental Emmental | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Draconic Depths | Loot: |  |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| After losing his tenure at the School of Sorcery, the relentless and resourceful Mythweaver came to realize that fantasy alone would not be enough to finally defeat the pesky humans and their persistent MouseHunters. He realized that he would need something much bigger and more terrifying this time. He needed something that struck to the very core of human fear, that burrowed deep into the psyche of MouseHunters and awakened some primal terror that has remained dormant since the time of their most ancient ancestors. He needed something so great and terrible that it would instantly remind the humans of their lowly place in the hierarchy of species.<br>After much searching, he eventually discovered a book much older and more primitive than the folktales he had been rewriting thus far. This book described a very different world with very different masters from a time long before the age of humans. A time that was ruled by fear and ferociousness, where only the mightiest and most ruthless survived and thrived; a time when mercy, compassion, and civilization were concepts yet to be discovered. T'was the time of dragons!<br>The Mythweaver studied the Tale of the First Dragons extensively and learned a most shocking and fortuitous discovery: the age of dragons had not ended, it was merely awaiting the beginning of its next chapter! And so, with quill in hand, inhibitions unfettered, and delusions of grandeur abound, he set upon that most villainous deed of penning the next chapter himself.<br>He followed the directions spelled out in the book and it led him deep below the Folklore Forest to a place that time forgot: the Draconic Depths. Deep within these coarse, cacophonous caverns, the Mythweaver discovered the hidden home and true origin of all the dragons in Gnawnia. Every great dragon that he had studied from the world above could trace its origins back to these dark and dangerous depths. From the furious flames of Kalor'ignis to the frigid ferocity of Icewing and all the way back to the great Dragon mouse itself. All of these legendary beasts once called these depths their home.<br>Here the Mythweaver had everything he needed to amass the greatest and most terrible army of ancient monsters with which to lay waste to the human world above. So he used every ounce of his fabled magic to transform himself into the mightiest dragon of them all and named himself their Emperor. Now he intends to lead this legendary legion up to the surface and dispose of the human Kingdom of Gnawnia once and for all. That is, unless the strongest and bravest MouseHunters can face their fears and descend into these dreadful depths to put a stop to this heinous plan before it takes flight! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1209 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

Mythical Dragon Emperor is only attracted to Elemental Emmental.

## Hunting Strategy

The Mythical Dragon Emperor can only be attracted while hunting in an Elemental Dragons' Den.

Only Draconic power types are effective against Mythical Dragon Emperor.
All other types are ineffective.

## History and Trivia

- 13 August 2024: Mythical Dragon Emperor was released as part of Draconic Depths location.

# 🐭 Mythical Giant King

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

Mythical Giant King is the final boss mouse in Bountiful Beanstalk.

| After being foiled by the hunters of Gnawnia at the Table of Contents, the Mythweaver was forced to abandon his plan to rewrite the Book of Lore. But as the hunters celebrated prematurely, this brilliant baddie vowed never to rest until his conquest was realized and thus he began work on an even more dastardly plan. Rather than simply rewriting history, the Mythweaver would use his formidable magic to write himself directly into Gnawnia's most important tales and conquer them each from within!<br>And so he began with the beloved Tale of the Bountiful Beanstalk in which a young hero defeats a greedy giant and restores the honour and riches that were stolen from his family. But where the original giant failed, the Mythweaver would succeed by making himself into the mighty and malevolent Mythical Giant King!<br>Now the Castle in the Clouds that hovers above the Bountiful Beanstalk is ruled by the biggest, smartest, and most sinister Giant that it has ever known. From this elevated seat of power, the Mythweaver will crush the young hero who dares to climb his beanstalk and the collective consciousness of Gnawnia will never be the same again!<br>Perhaps the fearless MouseHunters of Gnawnia can somehow find a way to insert themselves into this story and, with cunning, bravery, and a lot of luck, ensure that the hero fulfills their destiny by endowing this tyrannical Giant with a steep fall from grace! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1143 |  |  |

## Hunting Strategy

Mythical Giant King is available at the end of the giant chase phase on Great Hall Floor in Bountiful Beanstalk.
It can be attracted by Standard Cheese, Beanster, Lavish Beanster, Leaping Lavish Beanster, and Royal Beanster.

Physical power type is very effective against Mythical Giant King.
All other types are ineffective.

Catching Mythical Giant King will cause the Hunter to be moved to back to the Beanstalk.

# 🐭 Mythweaver

> **Entity**: Mouse_lore

Mythweaver is a breed of mouse found in Table of Contents.

| Mythweaver |  |  |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Mouse Group: Storytellers (Folklore Masterminds) |  |  |  |
| Mouse Statistics |  |  |  |
| Points: | 1,000,000 | Gold: | 70,000 |
| Location & Attraction Info |  |  |  |
| Required Power Types: | Forgotten | Other Requirements: | 4,000+ words when writing |
| Cheese: | Final Draft Derby Cheese | Charm: | None |
| Locations: | Table of Contents | Loot: | See below |
| Links |  |  |  |
| Larry's Loot Lexicon: | MouseHunt Info Page | Image: | Image Link |
| Many a cautionary tale has been written about the dangers of hubris but few are as poignant or consequential as the one being penned by the Mythweaver.<br>This once meek and meager mouse began its story as a simple Bookborn willed into existence by the maniacal Zurreal the Eternal. There was something unique about this particular manifestation however and it caused Zurreal to take special notice. This Bookborn exhibited more intelligence, intuition, and understanding than the rest of its kind and so Zurreal decided to share small fragments of his immense knowledge with this curious creature. As the Bookborn consumed more knowledge, its abilities began to grow and so too did its usefulness. Zurreal recognized an opportunity to mold and shape this exceptional creation into a powerful assistant capable of expediting his plans to conquer humans and mice alike.<br>Unbeknownst to Zurreal, this creature was consuming knowledge at an unimaginable rate and before long, its intelligence and understanding had begun to surpass even its master's. This nameless creation eventually sought to have an identity of its own and, having developed a particular fascination with the countless volumes of folklore contained in the Crystal Library, began calling itself the Mythweaver. The Mythweaver began to recognize that he was merely a servant to an insane and tyrannical leader, a mere cog in a broken machine. And with that profound realization, this ethereal creature transformed into a unique and extraordinarily powerful mouse! On that day, the Mythweaver vowed to turn against his creator and become a true leader of mice; dedicated not only to their survival but to their supremacy in the land of Gnawnia!<br>So the Mythweaver scoured every inch of the great library in search of a means by which he could conquer the kingdom on behalf of his kind and one day he found what he had been searching for. Hidden inside a forgotten book on a forgotten shelf, the Mythweaver discovered a single page that Zurreal had missed all these years. This enchanted page was written by Zugzwang himself and on it he learned of a secret place of unprecedented power: the Folklore Forest!<br>He learned that deep in the Folklore Forest there exists a magical Book of Lore which contains the entire history of Gnawnia scribbled throughout its pages. What is special about this book however, is that if the words on these pages are changed then so too will history itself be changed. And thus began the Mythweaver's quest to rewrite history by writing the mice as the glorious champions and the humans as the vanquished foes! |  |  |  |
|  | Mouse ID#: 1113 |  |  |

## Cheese Preference

Mythweaver is only attracted to Final Draft Derby Cheese.

## Hunting Strategy

Mythweaver is only available when the stage is writing and the Hunter has written at least 4,000 words in the current writing session.

Forgotten power type is very effective against Mythweaver.
All other types are ineffective.

## Loot

Mythweaver can drop:

- Ancient Relic
- Condensed Creativity
- Divine Orb
- Gnawbel Prize for Literature
- Enchanted Page

With Leather Bound Cover, the following loot is added to the pool:

- Cleverness Clam
- Crop Coin
- Inspiration Ink
- Mythical Mulch
- Pond Penny
- Papyrus Seed
- Parable Papyrus

With Silver Quill and Golden Quill, the following loot is added to the pool:

- Rainbow Nib

## History and Trivia

- 26 July 2022: The Mythweaver was released as part of Table of Contents location.
- Mythweaver is listed as Very Naughty on Naughty List Printing Press Base image.
- 19 August 2025: Rainbow Nib is added to the loot of the Mythweaver.
